class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\operatorname\u003c/span\u003e{ACE}_T,\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\\]\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","along with expansion discovery cost, wall-clock cost, peak memory, proof length, and instability across seeds. Report components separately even when an aggregate score is used.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eCurrent Operational Baseline: Conservative Data-ATP Run\u003c/span\u003e}","","A console excerpt supplied for this draft shows the conservative run continuing to commit expansions rather than stalling at a static counter. Over the excerpt, the run moves from 13,630 committed expansions at 64,811 seconds to 13,930 committed expansions at 70,884 seconds: 300 additional committed expansions over 6,073 seconds (about 1.69 hours, averaging about 20.2 seconds per committed expansion across this particular mixed segment). The instantaneous rate is clearly not constant.","","The open-goal count changes from one to three near expansion 13,780 and then from three to two near expansion 13,890. This is worth preserving because it illustrates why open-goal count is not a monotone \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e``\u003c/span\u003edistance to proof\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\u0026#39;\u0026#39;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e metric. Opening subgoals can reflect decomposition of a promising branch, while later discharge can reduce the count. The run excerpt is therefore evidence of continuing computation and state change, not evidence that a proof is imminent.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ecenter\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003etabular\u003c/span\u003e}{@{}rrrr@{}}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\toprule\u003c/span\u003e","Committed expansions \u0026amp; Elapsed seconds \u0026amp; Open goals \u0026amp; Note\\\\","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\midrule\u003c/span\u003e","13,630 \u0026amp; 64,811 \u0026amp; 1 \u0026amp; excerpt start\\\\","13,770 \u0026amp; 65,048 \u0026amp; 1 \u0026amp; last shown one-goal point\\\\","13,780 \u0026amp; 65,098 \u0026amp; 3 \u0026amp; goal count increases\\\\","13,880 \u0026amp; 68,995 \u0026amp; 3 \u0026amp; three-goal region\\\\","13,890 \u0026amp; 69,177 \u0026amp; 2 \u0026amp; one goal discharged/removed\\\\","13,930 \u0026amp; 70,884 \u0026amp; 2 \u0026amp; excerpt end\\\\","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\bottomrule\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003etabular\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ecenter\u003c/span\u003e}","","For D-A-T-P-CREAT-01, this run should be archived with exact configuration, checkpoint, and breadcrumbs. It may later become a matched baseline, but its scientific role should not be retroactively changed after results are known.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eConjectures for Data-ATP\u003c/span\u003e}","","The following statements are intentionally separated from the proved results. Each receives its own page and an explicit near-term test. A failed test is scientifically useful: the point is to make the claims falsifiable before architecture is added merely because it sounds intelligent.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eInterior Creativity Optimum\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Data-ATP inverted-U conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:u\u003c/span\u003e}","For sufficiently difficult, route-blind (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eK_\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e0\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e) theorem families under a fixed finite resource protocol, Data-ATP\u0026#39;s expected creative yield \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\cY\u003c/span\u003e(c)\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e has an interior maximizer \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ec^\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\star\\in\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e0\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e)\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e over a suitably calibrated one-dimensional creativity path, rather than being maximized at both endpoints.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eWhy it is plausible.\u003c/span\u003e} Low exploration can overtrust an imperfect ranking; very high exploration can diffuse a fixed budget. The two mechanisms can produce opposite slopes at opposite ends.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} Run the Stage A five-point grid under identical budgets and seeds. Fit a response surface with uncertainty intervals. Predeclare what size of endpoint/interior difference counts as practically meaningful. Repeat on held-out targets before calling the curve general.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} A monotone or flat relationship on a sufficiently powered held-out benchmark refutes the conjecture for that benchmark and implementation.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eGuidance--Creativity Compensation\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Guidance--creativity compensation conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:guidance\u003c/span\u003e}","As the calibration and ranking quality of a learned legal-move model improves, the optimal amount of undirected counterfactual exploration decreases on average, while remaining strictly positive on theorem families for which the ranker retains nonzero tail error on proof-enabling actions.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eInterpretation.\u003c/span\u003e} Better intelligence should reduce the amount of insurance required against ranking mistakes, but a finite learned ranker can still assign a necessary action just outside the active candidate frontier.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} Cross guidance off/on with the creativity grid in Protocol~\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\ref\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eprot:pilot\u003c/span\u003e}. Compare the fitted maximizing \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ec\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e values and record successful proofs in which a counterfactual admission rescues an action outside the ordinary candidate cap.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} If better-guided conditions consistently require equal or greater undirected exploration, or if the interaction is absent across held-out theorem families, the proposed compensation relation is unsupported.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eStagnation-Triggered Adaptive Creativity\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Adaptive stagnation conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:adaptive\u003c/span\u003e}","On heterogeneous theorem families, a controller that increases exploration after well-defined stagnation signals and decreases exploration after verified progress achieves greater held-out anytime creative efficiency than every tested fixed-creativity configuration under the same global expansion and hardware protocol.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","Possible stagnation signals include repeated frontier states, low novelty in admitted actions, repeated failure of the top-ranked branch family, absence of new verified lemmas, or prolonged lack of goal reduction. \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e``\u003c/span\u003eProgress\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\u0026#39;\u0026#39;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e must be defined without peeking at a hidden reference proof.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} First identify the best fixed settings on calibration targets. Freeze them. Then compare against an adaptive controller whose trigger rules were also fixed before the test targets are revealed.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} If the adaptive controller cannot beat the best fixed baseline after accounting for its computational overhead, the conjecture fails for that controller and benchmark.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eVerified Lemma Reuse and Population Search\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Shared-lemma population conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:lemmas\u003c/span\u003e}","For theorem families containing reusable intermediate structure, a population of heterogeneous Data-ATP agents sharing a verifier-certified lemma pool achieves higher held-out creative yield or anytime proof efficiency than (i) the best isolated fixed agent and (ii) a run-level random mixture of the same agent profiles, under the same global committed-expansion budget.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","The random-mixture ceiling in Theorem~\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\ref\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ethm:mixture\u003c/span\u003e} explains why simple personality diversity is insufficient. The conjectured advantage requires interaction: one agent\u0026#39;s verified discovery changes what another agent can do. Large-library ATP research likewise shows that learned premise selection and useful-lemma mining can materially strengthen search \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\cite\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ekaliszyk2014\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ekaliszyk2015\u003c/span\u003e}.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} After the single-agent creativity protocol is stable, compare isolated profiles, a run-level mixture, and a genuinely shared verified-lemma population. Charge lemma construction and communication against the same global budget.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} No improvement beyond the best isolated agent or mixture after matched-budget accounting means the shared structure did not earn its architectural cost on that benchmark.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eProof Geometry and Locality\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Proof-geometric locality conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:geometry\u003c/span\u003e}","If a coordinate encoding of candidate proof actions is constructed so that geometric proximity is positively associated with proof-theoretic relevance, then a locality-preserving traversal or allocation rule can achieve lower expected discovery cost than an arbitrary enumeration under the same fixed budget on at least some nontrivial theorem families.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","This is the bridge between the current creativity program and the Hilbert-inspired Data-ATP search-geometry proposal. A space-filling curve preserves geometric locality; the unresolved problem is whether the encoding gives that geometry mathematical meaning.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} Freeze the same candidate-action set and compare random, lexicographic, conventional learned ranking, and locality-preserving traversal. Measure not only theorem success but neighborhood coherence, legal-action yield, useful lemma density, redundancy, and anytime efficiency.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} If coordinate proximity does not predict proof-theoretic relevance, a locality-preserving traversal is preserving the wrong geometry and should not be expected to help.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\subsection\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eCreativity and Transfer\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}[Moderate-creativity transfer conjecture]\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\label\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003econj:transfer\u003c/span\u003e}","Search configurations selected for moderate verified structural novelty on calibration theorem families generalize better to theorem families with novel premises or unfamiliar proof structure than configurations selected solely for in-distribution success rate, provided total training information and resources are held fixed.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econjecture\u003c/span\u003e}","","This is motivated by the possibility that controlled exploration encourages reusable stepping stones rather than only reproducing familiar trajectories. Modern theorem-proving benchmarks increasingly emphasize held-out or novel-premise generalization, making transfer a natural place to test the claim \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\cite\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eyang2023\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ebansal2019\u003c/span\u003e}.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eNear-term test.\u003c/span\u003e} Separate theorem families into calibration, validation, and untouched transfer sets. Select one configuration by success alone and another by a declared creative-yield criterion, then compare both once on the transfer set.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eFalsification.\u003c/span\u003e} If creativity-selected configurations fail to improve transfer, or improve only after target-family tuning, the conjecture is not supported.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eOptimization Strategy\u003c/span\u003e}","","After basic controls are validated, treat ATP creativity as a noisy black-box response surface. Let","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\\[\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\widehat\u003c/span\u003e{U}(u)\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\\]\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","model the chosen multi-objective utility on calibration data. Expensive trial regimes favor sample-efficient sequential optimization such as Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization or tree-structured Parzen estimation \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\cite\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esnoek2012\u003c/span\u003e,\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ebergstra2011\u003c/span\u003e}. However, a transparent coarse grid remains valuable initially because it reveals whether the instrumentation itself behaves monotonically in the intended control variables.","","The recommended sequence is:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e validate each control independently;","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e measure interactions on a small factorial grid;","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e fit uncertainty-aware response surfaces;","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e choose a candidate optimum using calibration/validation only;","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e evaluate once on untouched held-out theorem families;","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e publish or archive the full outcome distribution, not only successful examples.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","","A single winning seed is not a creativity result. A theorem-proof certificate establishes correctness of one proof; repeated matched trials establish a claim about a search policy.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eWhat Would Count as a Better Proof Searcher?\u003c/span\u003e}","","A Data-ATP configuration should be called better only relative to a declared objective. A strong research criterion is:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003equote\u003c/span\u003e}","Under fixed information, verifier, hardware, and budget, does the policy increase the probability and/or speed of discovering verifier-accepted proofs on held-out targets while preserving or improving proof quality, robustness, structural diversity, or reusable lemma production?","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003equote\u003c/span\u003e}","","The answer could be \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e``\u003c/span\u003eyes\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\u0026#39;\u0026#39;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e even if the resulting proofs are not maximally novel. Conversely, a highly novel but rarely successful policy may be interesting as a scientific probe but not as the best prover.","","This suggests maintaining at least two scoreboards:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eProof-search scoreboard:\u003c/span\u003e} success, expansion discovery cost, wall time, memory, robustness, proof quality.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textbf\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-mb\"\u003eCreative-discovery scoreboard:\u003c/span\u003e} normalized novelty, creative yield, anytime creative efficiency, reusable lemma yield, route diversity.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","","The best architecture may lie on a Pareto frontier rather than at the maximum of either scoreboard alone.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eResearch Commitments for the Next Data-ATP Iteration\u003c/span\u003e}","","Before the next creativity-enabled run, the project should commit to the following private experimental rules:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Creativity changes search allocation, never formal acceptance.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e The user-facing scalar control is not itself called measured creativity in the data analysis.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Total resources are frozen or explicitly charged; higher creativity does not silently buy more computation.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Reference-route information is separated into explicit information conditions \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eK_\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e0\u003c/span\u003e,K_\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e,K_\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Novelty is scored only on verifier-accepted, normalized proofs.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Seeds and target partitions are declared before outcomes are inspected.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Current \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\texttt\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esgrpcl\u003c/span\u003e} results are archived as baseline observations, not retrofitted into a matched experiment.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Failed searches, bounded unknowns, crashes, and replay events are part of the dataset.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Conjectures in this document remain conjectures until the declared test actually supports them.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\\item\u003c/span\u003e Architecture complexity must earn its way in through measured gains.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eenumerate\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eConclusion\u003c/span\u003e}","","The central idea can be stated without anthropomorphism. Formal proof search is a resource-allocation problem over a vast set of legal possibilities. Learning supplies a prior ordering of attention. Creativity controls how willing the system is to depart from that ordering, search alternative neighborhoods, construct intermediate lemmas, restart, or preserve multiple competing theories of the proof. Verification remains unchanged.","","The mathematical results in this draft already rule out several tempting mistakes. Maximum temperature need not be best. Entropy does not uniquely determine creativity. Novelty alone can select a worse prover. Randomly mixing creative personalities cannot outperform the best constituent in expectation unless the architecture adds something more. Adaptive policies are theoretically worth considering because they contain fixed policies as special cases, but their actual benefit remains empirical. Counterfactual exploration has a clean rescue mechanism when a proof-enabling action lies outside an ordinary candidate cap.","","The near-term Data-ATP question is therefore precise:","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ecenter\u003c/span\u003e}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\fcolorbox\u003c/span\u003e{DataBlue}{DataLight}{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\parbox\u003c/span\u003e{0.86\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textwidth\u003c/span\u003e}{\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\centering\\bfseries\u003c/span\u003e Which forms and amounts of structured exploration turn a fixed budget into better verified discovery?}}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\end\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ecenter\u003c/span\u003e}","The right answer may be moderate creativity, adaptive creativity, a portfolio of cooperating agents, learned proof geometry, or none of these on a given benchmark. The point of D-A-T-P-CREAT-01 is to make that answer something the system can measure rather than something the architecture assumes.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\clearpage\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\appendix\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\section\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eMetric Summary\u003c/span\u003e}","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\begin\u003c/span\u003e{\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003elongtable\u003c/span\u003e}{@{}p{0.25\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textwidth\u003c/span\u003e}p{0.27\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textwidth\u003c/span\u003e}p{0.40\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\textwidth\u003c/span\u003e}@{}}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\toprule\u003c/span\u003e","Quantity \u0026amp; Definition \u0026amp; Interpretation\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\\\\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\midrule\u003c/span\u003e","\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\endhead\u003c/span\u003e","Control index \u0026amp; Expected structural novelty among successful runs.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\\\\u003c/span\u003e","Creative yield \u0026amp; \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\\cY\u003c/span\u003e(u)=D(u)N(u)\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e \u0026amp; <div id="LC72" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\Huge\bfseries</span> Creativity in Automated Theorem Proving<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.18in]}</div>
<div id="LC73" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\Large</span> A Data-ATP Experimental Treatise<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.16in]}</div>
<div id="LC74" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\large</span> Definitions, Optimization, Theorems, Conjectures, and Near-Term Tests<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.5in]}</div>
<div id="LC75" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\color</span>{DataBlue}<span class="pl-c1">\rule</span>{0.9<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}{1.4pt}}<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.65in]</div>
<div id="LC76" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC77" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\Large</span> Brian Tenneson}<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.12in]</div>
<div id="LC78" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\large</span> Private Research Draft -- Version 0.1}<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.12in]</div>
<div id="LC79" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\large</span> August 8, 2026}<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.65in]</div>
<div id="LC80" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC81" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\fcolorbox</span>{DataBlue}{DataLight}{<span class="pl-c1">\parbox</span>{0.84<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}{</div>
<div id="LC82" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Status.</span>} This document is a private experimental research draft. It is intended to turn the current ATP-creativity framework into testable Data-ATP protocols. The proved statements below are mathematical results under explicitly stated stylized assumptions; the conjectures are empirical or architectural claims that require experiments. Nothing labeled a conjecture is presented as established fact.</div>
<div id="LC83" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">}}<span class="pl-c1">\\</span>[0.7in]</div>
<div id="LC84" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC85" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\vfill</span></div>
<div id="LC86" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">{<span class="pl-c1">\small\textcolor</span>{DataGray}{Source-driven LaTeX manuscript. Bibliographic metadata review completed August 8, 2026.}}</div>
<div id="LC87" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">titlepage</span>}</div>
<div id="LC88" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\pagenumbering</span>{roman}</div>
<div id="LC89" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC90" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">abstract</span>}</div>
<div id="LC91" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Automated theorem proving has an asymmetry at its core: formal verification can determine whether a proposed certificate is correct, while search must decide which of an enormous number of possible legal constructions deserve finite computational attention. This manuscript develops a testable theory of <span class="pl-c1">\emph</span>{<span class="pl-mi">creativity in an automated theorem prover</span>} around that distinction. Creativity is operationalized as verified structural novelty under frozen information, formal environment, training, and computational-resource conditions. Exploratory controls---temperature, candidate width, breadth-depth allocation, novelty preference, restarts, lemma construction, and proof-length tolerance---are treated as inputs rather than creativity itself. Measured creative performance is instead an empirical outcome combining probability of verified discovery with normalized structural novelty.</div>
<div id="LC92" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC93" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The manuscript extends the existing ATP Creativity framework with ten proved results under explicit assumptions. These include a creative-yield factorization theorem, verifier-separation theorem, a theorem showing that increasing softmax temperature can strictly reduce creative yield, a same-entropy/different-creativity theorem, a novelty-only optimization failure theorem, a counterfactual-rescue theorem, a random-mixture ceiling theorem, an adaptive-policy feasible-set dominance theorem, an anytime-creative-efficiency identity, and a fixed-target scarcity-weighting equivalence theorem. Claims that require genuine empirical evidence---including an inverted-U creativity curve, adaptive creativity superiority, shared-lemma population gains, and proof-geometric locality---are isolated as conjectures, each with an explicit falsification-oriented Data-ATP test plan.</div>
<div id="LC94" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC95" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A near-term experimental program, DATP-CREAT-01, is specified in stages. The current long-running conservative Data-ATP search is documented as an operational baseline, not yet as evidence for or against a creativity hypothesis. The central research question is not whether an ATP can be made maximally exploratory, but whether a controlled amount and form of structured exploration can convert a fixed resource budget into more efficient, robust, novel, and transferable verified mathematical discovery.</div>
<div id="LC96" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">abstract</span>}</div>
<div id="LC97" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC98" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC99" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\tableofcontents</span></div>
<div id="LC100" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC101" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\pagenumbering</span>{arabic}</div>
<div id="LC102" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC103" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Purpose and Scope</span>}</div>
<div id="LC104" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC105" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The immediate purpose of this document is experimental: to write down, before the next generation of Data-ATP experiments, what is meant by creativity, what quantities should be optimized, what can already be proved mathematically about the proposed metrics, and what must remain conjectural until matched trials are run.</div>
<div id="LC106" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC107" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The starting point is the framework developed in the private ATP Creativity manuscripts: creativity is not an unanalyzed faculty but a controlled property of proof search, with verification deliberately kept separate from search policy <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC2</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonCombined</span>}. The same framework distinguishes an exploratory control index from observed creative performance and recommends repeated seeded trials, held-out theorem families, and sample-efficient optimization rather than optimizing a user-facing <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>creativity knob<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> directly <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC2</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonCombined</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC108" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC109" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This document makes a sharper three-way distinction:</div>
<div id="LC110" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC111" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\boxed</span>{</span></div>
<div id="LC112" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{exploratory control}</span></div>
<div id="LC113" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-cce">\;</span><span class="pl-c1">\neq</span><span class="pl-cce">\;</span></span></div>
<div id="LC114" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{realized creativity}</span></div>
<div id="LC115" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-cce">\;</span><span class="pl-c1">\neq</span><span class="pl-cce">\;</span></span></div>
<div id="LC116" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{overall proof-search quality}.}</span></div>
<div id="LC117" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC118" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC119" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A prover may be highly exploratory and accomplish little. A prover may be highly reliable yet reconstruct conventional routes. A prover may be genuinely creative but too expensive or unstable for a production role. The experimental goal is to discover which forms of exploration improve the objective actually declared for Data-ATP.</div>
<div id="LC120" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC121" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Foundational Separation: Truth Versus Attention</span>}</div>
<div id="LC122" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC123" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Formal automated reasoning has long separated proof procedures from the semantic or syntactic standards that make inferences valid; practical ATP engineering then adds search heuristics because legal search spaces become combinatorial <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">fitting1996</span>,<span class="pl-c1">harrison2009</span>}. Proof-complexity work further emphasizes that the existence of a proof and the resources needed to represent or discover a proof are distinct questions <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">cookreckhow1979</span>}. Machine-learning-guided systems such as ENIGMA, DeepMath, DeepHOL/HOList, GPT-f, and LeanDojo place learned nonuniform priorities on candidate proof actions or premises without redefining formal correctness <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">jakubuv2017</span>,<span class="pl-c1">alemi2016</span>,<span class="pl-c1">bansal2019</span>,<span class="pl-c1">polu2020</span>,<span class="pl-c1">yang2023</span>}. This is the architectural location in which the present notion of creativity belongs.</div>
<div id="LC124" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC125" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">definition</span>}[Verifier-separated ATP]</div>
<div id="LC126" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A verifier-separated ATP consists of a search mechanism that proposes candidate proof actions or certificates and a sound formal verifier <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> that alone determines acceptance. Search may change ranking, sampling, branching, restarts, lemma proposals, or resource allocation. It does not alter the axioms, inference rules, side conditions, or acceptance standard.</div>
<div id="LC127" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">definition</span>}</div>
<div id="LC128" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC129" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The guiding sentence is therefore:</div>
<div id="LC130" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC131" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\boxed</span>{<span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{Verification determines correctness; search determines attention.}}</span></div>
<div id="LC132" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC133" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC134" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This matters because it permits aggressive experimental search without allowing <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>creativity<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> to become a euphemism for invalid inference. The HaloProof creativity-control notes state this division explicitly: creativity may alter which legal actions are attempted, but not substitution rules, distinct-variable requirements, axioms, definitions, or final verification <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonHalo</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC135" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC136" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Experimental Definition of ATP Creativity</span>}</div>
<div id="LC137" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC138" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">definition</span>}[Controlled ATP creativity]</div>
<div id="LC139" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Fix a formal system <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>F<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, hypotheses <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, target theorem <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, verifier <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, information condition <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>K<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, training exposure <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>p<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, and finite resource protocol <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>T<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> denote a search-policy configuration. ATP creativity is operationalized as the production, under these frozen conditions, of a verifier-accepted proof whose normalized structure is novel relative to a predeclared comparison class.</div>
<div id="LC140" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">definition</span>}</div>
<div id="LC141" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC142" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This definition requires three protections.</div>
<div id="LC143" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC144" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC145" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Verification protection.</span>} Invalid mathematics receives no creativity credit.</div>
<div id="LC146" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Novelty protection.</span>} Syntactic padding, redundant detours, duplicate steps, irrelevant permutations, or merely renaming a familiar proof do not count as structural novelty.</div>
<div id="LC147" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Control protection.</span>} A supposed creativity improvement may not be purchased by giving one condition more training data, target leakage, theorem-library access, expansions, memory, wall-clock privilege, or weaker checking.</div>
<div id="LC148" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC149" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC150" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Candidate novelty features can include normalized proof-DAG distance, changed theorem dependencies, alternative decompositions, newly constructed reusable lemmas, different explicit maps or constructions, bypass of expected lemmas, and diversity across independent seeds <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonCombined</span>}. Novelty search and quality-diversity research provide useful external analogues: diversity can be an explicit search objective, but diversity alone need not equal quality <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">lehman2011</span>,<span class="pl-c1">pugh2016</span>,<span class="pl-c1">mouret2015</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC151" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC152" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Controls Are Inputs, Not Creativity</span>}</div>
<div id="LC153" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC154" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let the raw control vector be</div>
<div id="LC155" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC156" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">u=(<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>,w,b,n,r,<span class="pl-c1">\lambda</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\ell</span>),</span></div>
<div id="LC157" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC158" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">where <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> is action-selection temperature, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>w<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> candidate width, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>b<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> breadth-versus-depth allocation, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>n<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> novelty pressure, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>r<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> restart or abandonment behavior, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\lambda</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> willingness to construct intermediate lemmas, and <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\ell</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> proof-length tolerance or penalty.</div>
<div id="LC159" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC160" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A formal-system-relative scalar</div>
<div id="LC161" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC162" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">c_F(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_i <span class="pl-c1">\alpha</span>_{i,F}z_{i,F}(u_i),</span></div>
<div id="LC163" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\qquad</span></span></div>
<div id="LC164" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\alpha</span>_{i,F}<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span> 0,</span></div>
<div id="LC165" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\qquad</span></span></div>
<div id="LC166" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_i<span class="pl-c1">\alpha</span>_{i,F}=1,</span></div>
<div id="LC167" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC168" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">may summarize how exploratory the configuration is. Behavioral normalization is preferable to raw-parameter normalization because identical numerical values can induce different behavior in different calculi or theorem libraries <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC2</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC169" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC170" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">But <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>c_F(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> is not an observed creativity score. It is a compact description of the inputs.</div>
<div id="LC171" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC172" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Realized creative return</span>}</div>
<div id="LC173" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC174" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For a fixed target and budget, let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>S_u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be the event that policy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> finds a verifier-accepted proof. If successful, let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>P_u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be the normalized proof and let</div>
<div id="LC175" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC176" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)<span class="pl-c1">\in</span>[0,1]</span></div>
<div id="LC177" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC178" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">be its predeclared structural-novelty score. Define <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> on failure for notational convenience. The <span class="pl-c1">\emph</span>{<span class="pl-mi">realized creative return</span>} is</div>
<div id="LC179" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC180" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">Z_u=<span class="pl-c1">\ind</span>_{S_u}<span class="pl-cce">\,</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u),</span></div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC181" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC182" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and the <span class="pl-c1">\emph</span>{<span class="pl-mi">expected creative yield</span>} is</div>
<div id="LC183" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC184" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[Z_u].</span></div>
<div id="LC185" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC186" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC187" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Define also</div>
<div id="LC188" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC189" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">D(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\Prb</span>(S_u)</span></div>
<div id="LC190" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC191" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and, when <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(u)&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC192" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC193" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">N(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)<span class="pl-c1">\mid</span> S_u].</span></div>
<div id="LC194" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC195" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">If <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(u)=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, set <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(u)=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>.</div>
<div id="LC196" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC197" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Proved Results About Creativity Metrics</span>}</div>
<div id="LC198" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC199" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The theorems in this section are not empirical claims about Data-ATP. They are mathematical consequences of the definitions and stylized search models stated here. Their purpose is to prevent experimental interpretation errors before expensive trials are run.</div>
<div id="LC200" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC201" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Creative-yield factorization</span>}</div>
<div id="LC202" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC203" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Creative-yield factorization]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:factor</span>}</div>
<div id="LC204" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For every policy configuration <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC205" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC206" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)=D(u)N(u).</span></div>
<div id="LC207" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC208" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC209" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC210" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC211" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">If <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(u)=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, both sides are zero by definition. If <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(u)&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, then</div>
<div id="LC212" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC213" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC214" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\ind</span>_{S_u}<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)]</span></div>
<div id="LC215" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\Prb</span>(S_u)<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)<span class="pl-c1">\mid</span> S_u]</span></div>
<div id="LC216" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=D(u)N(u),</span></div>
<div id="LC217" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC218" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">by conditioning on the success event.</div>
<div id="LC219" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC220" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC221" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC222" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The product is useful precisely because it punishes two different failure modes. If novelty is high but success is nearly impossible, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> drives the score down. If success is reliable but the system only reconstructs familiar proofs, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> drives the score down.</div>
<div id="LC223" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC224" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC225" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Verifier separation</span>}</div>
<div id="LC226" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC227" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Search-policy soundness invariance]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:verifier</span>}</div>
<div id="LC228" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Assume <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> is sound for <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>(F,<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> in the sense that whenever <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> accepts a certificate <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>P<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> for <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, one has <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span><span class="pl-c1">\vdash</span>_F<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. If every ATP condition accepts a target only through <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, then changing the search policy, creativity controls, sampling distribution, agent profile, or ranking function cannot cause acceptance of a non-theorem.</div>
<div id="LC229" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC230" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC231" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC232" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let an arbitrary search policy emit a certificate <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>P<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> that is accepted. By hypothesis, acceptance occurs only because <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> accepts <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>P<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. By soundness of <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span><span class="pl-c1">\vdash</span>_F<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. The argument does not depend on how <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>P<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> was proposed. Therefore replacing one search policy by another changes discovery behavior but not the soundness implication from acceptance to theoremhood.</div>
<div id="LC233" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC234" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC235" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This is the formal reason creativity should be allowed to act on search but not on verification. A related verifier-separation proposition already appears in the Version 47 ATP analysis <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonDepths47</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC236" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC237" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">More exploration can strictly hurt</span>}</div>
<div id="LC238" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC239" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[High-temperature counterexample]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:temperature</span>}</div>
<div id="LC240" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">There exists a finite verifier-separated proof-search problem in which increasing softmax temperature strictly increases exploratory dispersion while strictly decreasing both proof success probability and expected creative yield.</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC241" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC242" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC243" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC244" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Consider a one-step budget with exactly two legal actions, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>b<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Action <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> immediately yields a verified proof of novelty <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_<span class="pl-c1">0</span>&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>; action <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>b<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> exhausts the budget without proof. Let the learned scores satisfy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>s_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>&gt;s_b<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and define <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\Delta</span>=s_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>-s_b&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Under softmax temperature <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC245" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC246" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">p_<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>(a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>)=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{e^{s_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>/<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>}}{e^{s_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>/<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>}+e^{s_b/<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>}}</span></div>
<div id="LC247" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{1+e^{-<span class="pl-c1">\Delta</span>/<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>}}.</span></div>
<div id="LC248" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC249" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Because <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>x(<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>)=<span class="pl-c1">\Delta</span>/<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> is strictly decreasing and the logistic function is strictly increasing,</div>
<div id="LC250" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC251" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{d}{d<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>}p_<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>(a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>)&lt;0.</span></div>
<div id="LC252" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC253" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hence <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>)=p_<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>(a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> strictly decreases. Since the only successful proof has novelty <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC254" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC255" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>)=<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_0p_<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>(a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>)</span></div>
<div id="LC256" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC257" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">also strictly decreases. Meanwhile the binary action distribution approaches <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>/<span class="pl-c1">2</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>/<span class="pl-c1">2</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> as <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> increases, so its Shannon entropy increases whenever <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>p_<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>(a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>)&gt;<span class="pl-c1">1</span>/<span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Thus greater temperature and greater action entropy can coincide with worse proof search and worse creative yield.</div>
<div id="LC258" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC259" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC260" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">corollary</span>}</div>
<div id="LC261" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">There is no general theorem that <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>maximum exploration<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> or <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>maximum temperature<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> is the optimal creativity setting for an ATP.</div>
<div id="LC262" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">corollary</span>}</div>
<div id="LC263" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC264" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Entropy cannot define creativity by itself</span>}</div>
<div id="LC265" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC266" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Same entropy, different creative yield]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:entropy</span>}</div>
<div id="LC267" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">There exist two policies with identical action entropy but different proof success probabilities and different creative yields.</div>
<div id="LC268" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC269" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC270" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC271" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Use the same one-step two-action problem as in Theorem~<span class="pl-k">\ref</span>{<span class="pl-c1">thm:temperature</span>}. Let policy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>A<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> choose the proving action <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> with probability <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>p<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, where <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>p<span class="pl-c1">\in</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>/<span class="pl-c1">2</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, and let policy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>B<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> choose <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> with probability <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>-p<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. The binary entropy</div>
<div id="LC272" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC273" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">H(p)=-p<span class="pl-c1">\log</span> p-(1-p)<span class="pl-c1">\log</span>(1-p)</span></div>
<div id="LC274" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC275" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">is symmetric, so <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>H(p)=H(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>-p)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Yet</div>
