<div id="LC276" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC277" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">D_A=p&gt;1-p=D_B,</span></div>
<div id="LC278" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC279" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and with proof novelty <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_<span class="pl-c1">0</span>&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC280" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC281" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_A=<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_0p&gt;<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_0(1-p)=<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_B.</span></div>
<div id="LC282" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC283" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Thus equal behavioral entropy need not imply equal creativity.</div>
<div id="LC284" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC285" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC286" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Entropy remains useful as a diagnostic of realized diversity, but it cannot replace outcome measurement.</div>
<div id="LC287" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC288" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Novelty alone can select the worse prover</span>}</div>
<div id="LC289" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC290" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Conditional-novelty optimization failure]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:noveltytrap</span>}</div>
<div id="LC291" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">There exist ATP policies <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>A<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>B<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> such that <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(B)&gt;N(A)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> but <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(B)&lt;<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(A)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>.</div>
<div id="LC292" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC293" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC294" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC295" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Choose any <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>&lt;n_A&lt;n_B<span class="pl-c1">\le</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(A)=<span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and choose <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(B)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> such that</div>
<div id="LC296" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC297" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">0&lt;D(B)&lt;<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{n_A}{n_B}.</span></div>
<div id="LC298" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC299" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Set <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(A)=n_A<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(B)=n_B<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Then <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(B)&gt;N(A)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, but</div>
<div id="LC300" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC301" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(B)=D(B)n_B&lt;n_A=D(A)n_A=<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(A).</span></div>
<div id="LC302" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC303" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC304" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC305" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Thus optimizing novelty conditional on success can prefer a system that almost never proves anything. This is a reason to optimize a joint outcome such as <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>DN<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> rather than novelty alone.</div>
<div id="LC306" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC307" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Counterfactual rescue</span>}</div>
<div id="LC308" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC309" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Counterfactual-rescue lower bound]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:rescue</span>}</div>
<div id="LC310" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Suppose a proof-search state <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>S<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> has a unique proof-enabling first action <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> within the remaining budget. A deterministic candidate-cap policy admits only ranks <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\dots</span>,k<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, while <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> has rank <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>r&gt;k<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Then the capped policy has success probability zero. If a counterfactual policy selects <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> with probability at least <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>p&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and, conditional on that selection, completes a verified proof within budget with probability at least <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>q&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, then</div>
<div id="LC311" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC312" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">D_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{cf}}<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span> pq.</span></div>
<div id="LC313" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC314" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">If every such completed proof has novelty at least <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_<span class="pl-c1">0</span>&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, then</div>
<div id="LC315" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC316" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{cf}}<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span> pq<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_0&gt;0.</span></div>
<div id="LC317" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC318" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC319" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC320" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC321" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The deterministic cap never admits <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, so by the uniqueness assumption no within-budget proof can be reached and its success probability is zero. Under counterfactual selection, the event <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>select <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>a_<span class="pl-c1">\star</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and then complete the proof<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> has probability at least <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>pq<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, yielding <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{cf}}<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span> pq<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Multiplying by the lower novelty bound gives the creative-yield inequality.</div>
<div id="LC322" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC323" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC324" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The theorem captures the mechanism seen in the controlled <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">prcom</span>} proof-of-concept: learned ranking moved a useful assertion substantially upward, but counterfactual admission was still needed to cross the candidate cap <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonDepths47</span>}. That finite-instance result motivates, but does not itself prove, a general creativity advantage.</div>
<div id="LC325" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC326" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">A random portfolio is not automatically better</span>}</div>
<div id="LC327" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC328" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Random-mixture ceiling]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:mixture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC329" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>_<span class="pl-c1">1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\dots</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>_m<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be fixed full-budget policies with expected creative yields <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_<span class="pl-c1">1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\dots</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_m<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. Consider a run-level mixture that, before search begins, chooses policy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>_i<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> with probability <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>w_i<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, where <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>w_i<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_iw_i=<span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, and then runs only that policy with no adaptation or information sharing. Then</div>
<div id="LC330" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC331" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{mix}}=<span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_{i=1}^m w_i<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_i</span></div>
<div id="LC332" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\le</span> <span class="pl-c1">\max</span>_i<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_i.</span></div>
<div id="LC333" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC334" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC335" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC336" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC337" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">By the law of total expectation conditioned on the selected policy,</div>
<div id="LC338" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC339" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{mix}}</span></div>
<div id="LC340" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_i w_i<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[Z<span class="pl-c1">\mid\pi</span>_i]</span></div>
<div id="LC341" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_iw_i<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_i.</span></div>
<div id="LC342" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC343" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A convex combination cannot exceed its largest component.</div>
<div id="LC344" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC345" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC346" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC347" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This theorem is deliberately useful for population design. A population is not guaranteed to help merely because it contains different personalities. To beat the random-mixture ceiling in expected creative yield, it must exploit something beyond pre-run randomization: state-dependent specialization, resource reallocation, target conditioning, shared verified lemmas, cooperative search, different risk objectives, or some other within-run interaction.</div>
<div id="LC348" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC349" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC350" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Why adaptive creativity is worth testing</span>}</div>
<div id="LC351" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC352" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Adaptive-policy feasible-set dominance]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:adaptive</span>}</div>
<div id="LC353" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\PiFix</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be a class of fixed-control policies and let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\PiAd</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be an adaptive class that contains every fixed policy as the special case of a controller that never changes its controls. For any expected utility functional <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>U(<span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> defined on both classes,</div>
<div id="LC354" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC355" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\sup</span>_{<span class="pl-c1">\pi\in\PiAd</span>}U(<span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>)</span></div>
<div id="LC356" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\ge</span></span></div>
<div id="LC357" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\sup</span>_{<span class="pl-c1">\pi\in\PiFix</span>}U(<span class="pl-c1">\pi</span>).</span></div>
<div id="LC358" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC359" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC360" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC361" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC362" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Because <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\PiFix</span><span class="pl-c1">\subseteq</span><span class="pl-c1">\PiAd</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, optimization over the larger feasible set cannot have a smaller supremum than optimization over the subset.</div>
<div id="LC363" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC364" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC365" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC366" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This is a possibility theorem, not an empirical superiority theorem. Finite data, controller overhead, miscalibration, or overfitting may cause an implemented adaptive system to perform worse. The theorem only says that a correctly defined adaptive policy class does not lose the best fixed policies from its theoretical option set.</div>
<div id="LC367" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">remark</span>}</div>
<div id="LC368" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC369" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Rewarding earlier creative discovery</span>}</div>
<div id="LC370" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC371" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\in</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\infty</span>]<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> denote the expansion count or time at which the first accepted proof is discovered, with <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u=<span class="pl-c1">\infty</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> on failure. Let <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\in</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>]<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be the novelty of that first proof, and set <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> on failure. Define the time-indexed creative yield</div>
<div id="LC372" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC373" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_u(t)=<span class="pl-c1">\E\left</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\ind</span>_{<span class="pl-cce">\{</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\le</span> t<span class="pl-cce">\}</span>}<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>].</span></div>
<div id="LC374" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC375" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For a horizon <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>T&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, define the <span class="pl-c1">\emph</span>{<span class="pl-mi">Anytime Creative Efficiency</span>}</div>
<div id="LC376" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC377" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\operatorname</span>{ACE}_T(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC378" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{T}<span class="pl-c1">\int</span>_0^T<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_u(t)<span class="pl-cce">\,</span>dt.</span></div>
<div id="LC379" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC380" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC381" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Anytime creative-efficiency identity]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:ace</span>}</div>
<div id="LC382" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For every policy <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> and horizon <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>T&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC383" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC384" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\operatorname</span>{ACE}_T(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC385" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{T}<span class="pl-c1">\E\left</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u(T-<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u)_+<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>],</span></div>
<div id="LC386" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC387" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">where <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>(x)_+=<span class="pl-c1">\max</span><span class="pl-c1">\{</span>x,<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-cce">\}</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>.</div>
<div id="LC388" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC389" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC390" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC391" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Because the integrand is nonnegative, Tonelli&#39;s theorem permits interchange of expectation and integration:</div>
<div id="LC392" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">align*</span>}<span class="pl-s"></span></div>
<div id="LC393" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\operatorname</span>{ACE}_T(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC394" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">&amp;=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{T}<span class="pl-c1">\int</span>_0^T<span class="pl-c1">\E\left</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\ind</span>_{<span class="pl-cce">\{</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\le</span> t<span class="pl-cce">\}</span>}<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>]dt<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></span></div>
<div id="LC395" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">&amp;=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{T}<span class="pl-c1">\E\left</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\int</span>_0^T<span class="pl-c1">\ind</span>_{<span class="pl-cce">\{</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u<span class="pl-c1">\le</span> t<span class="pl-cce">\}</span>}dt<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>]<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></span></div>
<div id="LC396" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">&amp;=<span class="pl-c1">\frac</span>{1}{T}<span class="pl-c1">\E\left</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u(T-<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u)_+<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>].</span></div>
<div id="LC397" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"></span><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">align*</span>}</div>
<div id="LC398" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC399" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC400" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This metric distinguishes <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>found at expansion 2,000<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> from <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>found at expansion 79,999<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> even when both runs ultimately succeed inside an 80,000-expansion budget.</div>
<div id="LC401" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC402" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Scarcity weighting does not change a fixed-target optimum</span>}</div>
<div id="LC403" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC404" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Let a target-relative scarcity weight <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F(<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>)<span class="pl-c1">\ge</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> be fixed independently of policy, as in the ATP Creativity proof-space framework <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonCombined</span>}. Define</div>
<div id="LC405" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC406" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_F^<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>(u)=R_F(<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>)<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u).</span></div>
<div id="LC407" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC408" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC409" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}[Fixed-target scarcity equivalence]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">thm:scarcity</span>}</div>
<div id="LC410" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For a fixed target with <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F(<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>)&gt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>,</div>
<div id="LC411" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC412" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\arg\max</span>_u <span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>_F^<span class="pl-c1">\star</span>(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC413" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">=</span></div>
<div id="LC414" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\arg\max</span>_u <span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u).</span></div>
<div id="LC415" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC416" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">If <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F(<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>)=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, then every policy has weighted score zero.</div>
<div id="LC417" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">theorem</span>}</div>
<div id="LC418" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC419" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC420" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For positive constant <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, multiplication by <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> preserves all order comparisons among the values <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>. If <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F=<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>, the weighted objective is identically zero.</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC421" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">proof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC422" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC423" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Scarcity weighting therefore matters primarily when aggregating across targets; it is not a reason to change which policy wins a matched experiment on one fixed theorem.</div>
<div id="LC424" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC425" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">From Creativity to Better Proof Search</span>}</div>
<div id="LC426" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC427" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A high creativity score is not automatically the same as a better ATP. Let</div>
<div id="LC428" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC429" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>S(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> denote verified success rate,</div>
<div id="LC430" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>K(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> normalized computational cost,</div>
<div id="LC431" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>V(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> run-to-run instability,</div>
<div id="LC432" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>Q(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> proof quality or simplicity,</div>
<div id="LC433" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>C(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> benchmark creative performance.</div>
<div id="LC434" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC435" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A declared system utility might take the form</div>
<div id="LC436" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC437" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">U(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\beta</span>_C C(u)+<span class="pl-c1">\beta</span>_SS(u)+<span class="pl-c1">\beta</span>_QQ(u)-<span class="pl-c1">\beta</span>_KK(u)-<span class="pl-c1">\beta</span>_VV(u),</span></div>
<div id="LC438" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC439" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with coefficients determined by the prover&#39;s intended role <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC2</span>}. For a research prover, novel mathematics may be highly valued. For a production verifier, reliability and cost may dominate. A Pareto frontier is often more scientifically transparent than pretending there is a universal scalar definition of <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>best.<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span></div>
<div id="LC440" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC441" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The external quality-diversity literature is relevant here because it explicitly studies the joint production of diverse and high-performing solutions rather than diversity alone <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">pugh2016</span>,<span class="pl-c1">mouret2015</span>}. In ATPs, the analogy must be made carefully: formal proof validity remains a hard acceptance constraint, while diversity applies to verified routes, intermediate structures, or search behavior.</div>
<div id="LC442" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC443" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">The Creativity Curve as an Empirical Object</span>}</div>
<div id="LC444" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC445" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The current private framework predicts a plausible inverted-U relationship: very low exploration may overexploit familiar routes; moderate exploration may rescue ranking mistakes and discover reusable lemmas; very high exploration may waste a finite budget on poorly directed branching <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">tennesonC1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">tennesonCombined</span>}. This is closely related to the general exploration--exploitation problem studied in reinforcement learning, although the ATP setting has the additional hard constraint of formal verification <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sutton2018</span>}. Theorem~<span class="pl-k">\ref</span>{<span class="pl-c1">thm:temperature</span>} proves only that <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>more exploration is always better<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> is false in general. It does not prove an inverted U.</div>
<div id="LC446" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC447" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The quantity to estimate is a response surface such as</div>
<div id="LC448" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC449" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">(u,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>,K,p,T)<span class="pl-c1">\longmapsto</span></span></div>
<div id="LC450" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\left</span>(D,N,<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\operatorname</span>{ACE},S,K_{<span class="pl-c1">\mathrm</span>{cost}},V,Q<span class="pl-c1">\right</span>).</span></div>
<div id="LC451" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC452" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Because ATP trials are noisy and expensive, sequential design methods such as Bayesian optimization and tree-structured Parzen estimators are natural candidates for tuning after a clean calibration/validation/test split is frozen <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">snoek2012</span>,<span class="pl-c1">bergstra2011</span>}. The optimizer should target observed outcome metrics, not merely the scalar control index.</div>
<div id="LC453" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC454" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Near-Term Data-ATP Experimental Program</span>}</div>
<div id="LC455" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC456" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">protocol</span>}[DATP-CREAT-01: Creativity isolation pilot]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">prot:pilot</span>}</div>
<div id="LC457" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first Data-ATP creativity experiment should use a deliberately simple, auditable matched design before population agents, dynamic geometry, or adaptive controllers are introduced.</div>
<div id="LC458" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">protocol</span>}</div>
<div id="LC459" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC460" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Stage A: control validation on inexpensive targets</span>}</div>
<div id="LC461" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC462" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Use theorem targets that can be run repeatedly at modest budgets. The first private pilot can include already familiar diagnostic targets such as <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">prcom</span>}, <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">prcon</span>}, and <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">0cn</span>}, supplemented by a predeclared frozen sample of additional pre-target theorems. The purpose is not to prove that these targets are difficult; it is to verify that creativity controls actually induce the intended behavioral changes and that logging is sufficient.</div>
<div id="LC463" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC464" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Freeze:</div>
<div id="LC465" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC466" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> formal database and declaration cutoff;</div>
<div id="LC467" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> target/downstream-proof exclusion rules;</div>
<div id="LC468" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> model architecture, weights, and preprocessing;</div>
<div id="LC469" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> verifier implementation and hash;</div>
<div id="LC470" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> hardware allocation;</div>
<div id="LC471" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> primary expansion budget;</div>
<div id="LC472" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> memory and wall-clock safety caps;</div>
<div id="LC473" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> maximum logical depth/open-goal constraints;</div>
<div id="LC474" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> proof normalization and novelty scoring rules;</div>
<div id="LC475" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> random-seed list before examining outcomes.</div>
<div id="LC476" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC477" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC478" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The first factorial grid should be</div>
<div id="LC479" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC480" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{guidance}<span class="pl-c1">\in</span><span class="pl-cce">\{</span><span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{off},<span class="pl-c1">\text</span>{on}<span class="pl-cce">\}</span></span></div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC481" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\quad\times\quad</span></span></div>
<div id="LC482" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">c<span class="pl-c1">\in</span><span class="pl-cce">\{</span>0,0.25,0.50,0.75,1.00<span class="pl-cce">\}</span>.</span></div>
<div id="LC483" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC484" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Use one search agent initially. A pilot of five seeds per cell is sufficient to debug the protocol; a larger repeated-seed study should follow only after the instrumentation is stable.</div>
<div id="LC485" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC486" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Stage B: transfer to <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sgrpcl</span>}</span>}</div>
<div id="LC487" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC488" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Do not tune on <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sgrpcl</span>} after inspecting its creativity outcomes. Select a small number of configurations from Stage A using predeclared criteria, freeze them, and then transfer them to <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sgrpcl</span>}. The present long-running conservative run can serve as an operational baseline if its exact configuration and environment are fully preserved, but it is not by itself a creativity comparison because no matched alternate-creativity condition has yet been run.</div>
<div id="LC489" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC490" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Stage C: harder transfer and HaloProof</span>}</div>
<div id="LC491" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC492" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">After the cheap-target and <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sgrpcl</span>} stages establish reproducibility, test the same mechanism on the substantially harder HaloProof benchmark. This is where lemma construction, structural novelty, and search-profile differences should become substantially more informative than on short routine targets.</div>
<div id="LC493" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC494" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">What to record</span>}</div>
<div id="LC495" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC496" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Every run should log at least:</div>
<div id="LC497" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC498" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> theorem and condition identifiers;</div>
<div id="LC499" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> raw and normalized creativity controls;</div>
<div id="LC500" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> seed and environment/verifier hashes;</div>
<div id="LC501" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> committed expansions and elapsed time;</div>
<div id="LC502" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> open-goal count and frontier size over time;</div>
<div id="LC503" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> candidate ranks before and after learned guidance;</div>
<div id="LC504" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> realized action entropy and candidate width;</div>
<div id="LC505" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> counterfactual admissions and whether they enter successful proofs;</div>
<div id="LC506" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> restarts and branch-abandonment events;</div>
<div id="LC507" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> attempted, verified, reused, and discarded lemmas;</div>
<div id="LC508" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> proof certificate and normalized proof DAG on success;</div>
<div id="LC509" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> checkpoint/replay overhead;</div>
<div id="LC510" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> clean distinction among verified success, bounded unknown, frontier exhaustion, and implementation failure.</div>
<div id="LC511" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">itemize</span>}</div>
<div id="LC512" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC513" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Primary estimands</span>}</div>
<div id="LC514" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC515" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For each target and condition estimate</div>
<div id="LC516" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC517" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s">D,<span class="pl-c1">\qquad</span> N,<span class="pl-c1">\qquad</span> <span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>=DN,<span class="pl-c1">\qquad</span> <span class="pl-c1">\operatorname</span>{ACE}_T,</span></div>
<div id="LC518" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC519" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">along with expansion discovery cost, wall-clock cost, peak memory, proof length, and instability across seeds. Report components separately even when an aggregate score is used.</div>
<div id="LC520" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC521" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Current Operational Baseline: Conservative Data-ATP Run</span>}</div>
<div id="LC522" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC523" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A console excerpt supplied for this draft shows the conservative run continuing to commit expansions rather than stalling at a static counter. Over the excerpt, the run moves from 13,630 committed expansions at 64,811 seconds to 13,930 committed expansions at 70,884 seconds: 300 additional committed expansions over 6,073 seconds (about 1.69 hours, averaging about 20.2 seconds per committed expansion across this particular mixed segment). The instantaneous rate is clearly not constant.</div>
<div id="LC524" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC525" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The open-goal count changes from one to three near expansion 13,780 and then from three to two near expansion 13,890. This is worth preserving because it illustrates why open-goal count is not a monotone <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>distance to proof<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> metric. Opening subgoals can reflect decomposition of a promising branch, while later discharge can reduce the count. The run excerpt is therefore evidence of continuing computation and state change, not evidence that a proof is imminent.</div>
<div id="LC526" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC527" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">center</span>}</div>
<div id="LC528" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">tabular</span>}{@{}rrrr@{}}</div>
<div id="LC529" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\toprule</span></div>
<div id="LC530" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Committed expansions &amp; Elapsed seconds &amp; Open goals &amp; Note\\</div>
<div id="LC531" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\midrule</span></div>
<div id="LC532" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,630 &amp; 64,811 &amp; 1 &amp; excerpt start\\</div>
<div id="LC533" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,770 &amp; 65,048 &amp; 1 &amp; last shown one-goal point\\</div>
<div id="LC534" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,780 &amp; 65,098 &amp; 3 &amp; goal count increases\\</div>
<div id="LC535" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,880 &amp; 68,995 &amp; 3 &amp; three-goal region\\</div>
<div id="LC536" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,890 &amp; 69,177 &amp; 2 &amp; one goal discharged/removed\\</div>
<div id="LC537" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">13,930 &amp; 70,884 &amp; 2 &amp; excerpt end\\</div>
<div id="LC538" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\bottomrule</span></div>
<div id="LC539" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">tabular</span>}</div>
<div id="LC540" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">center</span>}</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC541" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC542" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For D-A-T-P-CREAT-01, this run should be archived with exact configuration, checkpoint, and breadcrumbs. It may later become a matched baseline, but its scientific role should not be retroactively changed after results are known.</div>
<div id="LC543" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC544" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Conjectures for Data-ATP</span>}</div>
<div id="LC545" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC546" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The following statements are intentionally separated from the proved results. Each receives its own page and an explicit near-term test. A failed test is scientifically useful: the point is to make the claims falsifiable before architecture is added merely because it sounds intelligent.</div>
<div id="LC547" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC548" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC549" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Interior Creativity Optimum</span>}</div>
<div id="LC550" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC551" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Data-ATP inverted-U conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:u</span>}</div>
<div id="LC552" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For sufficiently difficult, route-blind (<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>K_<span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>) theorem families under a fixed finite resource protocol, Data-ATP&#39;s expected creative yield <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(c)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> has an interior maximizer <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>c^<span class="pl-c1">\star\in</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> over a suitably calibrated one-dimensional creativity path, rather than being maximized at both endpoints.</div>
<div id="LC553" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC554" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC555" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Why it is plausible.</span>} Low exploration can overtrust an imperfect ranking; very high exploration can diffuse a fixed budget. The two mechanisms can produce opposite slopes at opposite ends.</div>
<div id="LC556" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC557" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} Run the Stage A five-point grid under identical budgets and seeds. Fit a response surface with uncertainty intervals. Predeclare what size of endpoint/interior difference counts as practically meaningful. Repeat on held-out targets before calling the curve general.</div>
<div id="LC558" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC559" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} A monotone or flat relationship on a sufficiently powered held-out benchmark refutes the conjecture for that benchmark and implementation.</div>
<div id="LC560" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC561" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC562" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Guidance--Creativity Compensation</span>}</div>
<div id="LC563" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC564" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Guidance--creativity compensation conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:guidance</span>}</div>
<div id="LC565" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">As the calibration and ranking quality of a learned legal-move model improves, the optimal amount of undirected counterfactual exploration decreases on average, while remaining strictly positive on theorem families for which the ranker retains nonzero tail error on proof-enabling actions.</div>
<div id="LC566" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC567" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC568" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Interpretation.</span>} Better intelligence should reduce the amount of insurance required against ranking mistakes, but a finite learned ranker can still assign a necessary action just outside the active candidate frontier.</div>
<div id="LC569" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC570" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} Cross guidance off/on with the creativity grid in Protocol~<span class="pl-k">\ref</span>{<span class="pl-c1">prot:pilot</span>}. Compare the fitted maximizing <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>c<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> values and record successful proofs in which a counterfactual admission rescues an action outside the ordinary candidate cap.</div>
<div id="LC571" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC572" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} If better-guided conditions consistently require equal or greater undirected exploration, or if the interaction is absent across held-out theorem families, the proposed compensation relation is unsupported.</div>
<div id="LC573" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC574" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC575" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Stagnation-Triggered Adaptive Creativity</span>}</div>
<div id="LC576" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC577" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Adaptive stagnation conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:adaptive</span>}</div>
<div id="LC578" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On heterogeneous theorem families, a controller that increases exploration after well-defined stagnation signals and decreases exploration after verified progress achieves greater held-out anytime creative efficiency than every tested fixed-creativity configuration under the same global expansion and hardware protocol.</div>
<div id="LC579" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC580" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC581" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Possible stagnation signals include repeated frontier states, low novelty in admitted actions, repeated failure of the top-ranked branch family, absence of new verified lemmas, or prolonged lack of goal reduction. <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>Progress<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> must be defined without peeking at a hidden reference proof.</div>
<div id="LC582" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC583" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} First identify the best fixed settings on calibration targets. Freeze them. Then compare against an adaptive controller whose trigger rules were also fixed before the test targets are revealed.</div>
<div id="LC584" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC585" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} If the adaptive controller cannot beat the best fixed baseline after accounting for its computational overhead, the conjecture fails for that controller and benchmark.</div>
<div id="LC586" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC587" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC588" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Verified Lemma Reuse and Population Search</span>}</div>
<div id="LC589" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC590" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Shared-lemma population conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:lemmas</span>}</div>
<div id="LC591" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">For theorem families containing reusable intermediate structure, a population of heterogeneous Data-ATP agents sharing a verifier-certified lemma pool achieves higher held-out creative yield or anytime proof efficiency than (i) the best isolated fixed agent and (ii) a run-level random mixture of the same agent profiles, under the same global committed-expansion budget.</div>
<div id="LC592" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC593" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC594" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The random-mixture ceiling in Theorem~<span class="pl-k">\ref</span>{<span class="pl-c1">thm:mixture</span>} explains why simple personality diversity is insufficient. The conjectured advantage requires interaction: one agent&#39;s verified discovery changes what another agent can do. Large-library ATP research likewise shows that learned premise selection and useful-lemma mining can materially strengthen search <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">kaliszyk2014</span>,<span class="pl-c1">kaliszyk2015</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC595" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC596" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} After the single-agent creativity protocol is stable, compare isolated profiles, a run-level mixture, and a genuinely shared verified-lemma population. Charge lemma construction and communication against the same global budget.</div>
<div id="LC597" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC598" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} No improvement beyond the best isolated agent or mixture after matched-budget accounting means the shared structure did not earn its architectural cost on that benchmark.</div>
<div id="LC599" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC600" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC601" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Proof Geometry and Locality</span>}</div>
<div id="LC602" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC603" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Proof-geometric locality conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:geometry</span>}</div>
<div id="LC604" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">If a coordinate encoding of candidate proof actions is constructed so that geometric proximity is positively associated with proof-theoretic relevance, then a locality-preserving traversal or allocation rule can achieve lower expected discovery cost than an arbitrary enumeration under the same fixed budget on at least some nontrivial theorem families.</div>
<div id="LC605" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC606" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
