<div id="LC607" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This is the bridge between the current creativity program and the Hilbert-inspired Data-ATP search-geometry proposal. A space-filling curve preserves geometric locality; the unresolved problem is whether the encoding gives that geometry mathematical meaning.</div>
<div id="LC608" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC609" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} Freeze the same candidate-action set and compare random, lexicographic, conventional learned ranking, and locality-preserving traversal. Measure not only theorem success but neighborhood coherence, legal-action yield, useful lemma density, redundancy, and anytime efficiency.</div>
<div id="LC610" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC611" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} If coordinate proximity does not predict proof-theoretic relevance, a locality-preserving traversal is preserving the wrong geometry and should not be expected to help.</div>
<div id="LC612" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC613" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC614" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\subsection</span>{<span class="pl-en">Creativity and Transfer</span>}</div>
<div id="LC615" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC616" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}[Moderate-creativity transfer conjecture]<span class="pl-k">\label</span>{<span class="pl-v">conj:transfer</span>}</div>
<div id="LC617" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Search configurations selected for moderate verified structural novelty on calibration theorem families generalize better to theorem families with novel premises or unfamiliar proof structure than configurations selected solely for in-distribution success rate, provided total training information and resources are held fixed.</div>
<div id="LC618" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">conjecture</span>}</div>
<div id="LC619" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC620" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This is motivated by the possibility that controlled exploration encourages reusable stepping stones rather than only reproducing familiar trajectories. Modern theorem-proving benchmarks increasingly emphasize held-out or novel-premise generalization, making transfer a natural place to test the claim <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">yang2023</span>,<span class="pl-c1">bansal2019</span>}.</div>
<div id="LC621" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC622" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Near-term test.</span>} Separate theorem families into calibration, validation, and untouched transfer sets. Select one configuration by success alone and another by a declared creative-yield criterion, then compare both once on the transfer set.</div>
<div id="LC623" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC624" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Falsification.</span>} If creativity-selected configurations fail to improve transfer, or improve only after target-family tuning, the conjecture is not supported.</div>
<div id="LC625" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC626" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Optimization Strategy</span>}</div>
<div id="LC627" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC628" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">After basic controls are validated, treat ATP creativity as a noisy black-box response surface. Let</div>
<div id="LC629" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\[</span></span></div>
<div id="LC630" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-c1">\widehat</span>{U}(u)</span></div>
<div id="LC631" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">\]</span></span></div>
<div id="LC632" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">model the chosen multi-objective utility on calibration data. Expensive trial regimes favor sample-efficient sequential optimization such as Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization or tree-structured Parzen estimation <span class="pl-k">\cite</span>{<span class="pl-c1">snoek2012</span>,<span class="pl-c1">bergstra2011</span>}. However, a transparent coarse grid remains valuable initially because it reveals whether the instrumentation itself behaves monotonically in the intended control variables.</div>
<div id="LC633" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC634" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The recommended sequence is:</div>
<div id="LC635" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC636" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> validate each control independently;</div>
<div id="LC637" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> measure interactions on a small factorial grid;</div>
<div id="LC638" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> fit uncertainty-aware response surfaces;</div>
<div id="LC639" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> choose a candidate optimum using calibration/validation only;</div>
<div id="LC640" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> evaluate once on untouched held-out theorem families;</div>
<div id="LC641" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> publish or archive the full outcome distribution, not only successful examples.</div>
<div id="LC642" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC643" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC644" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A single winning seed is not a creativity result. A theorem-proof certificate establishes correctness of one proof; repeated matched trials establish a claim about a search policy.</div>
<div id="LC645" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC646" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">What Would Count as a Better Proof Searcher?</span>}</div>
<div id="LC647" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC648" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">A Data-ATP configuration should be called better only relative to a declared objective. A strong research criterion is:</div>
<div id="LC649" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">quote</span>}</div>
<div id="LC650" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Under fixed information, verifier, hardware, and budget, does the policy increase the probability and/or speed of discovering verifier-accepted proofs on held-out targets while preserving or improving proof quality, robustness, structural diversity, or reusable lemma production?</div>
<div id="LC651" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">quote</span>}</div>
<div id="LC652" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC653" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The answer could be <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">``</span>yes<span class="pl-pds">&#39;&#39;</span></span> even if the resulting proofs are not maximally novel. Conversely, a highly novel but rarely successful policy may be interesting as a scientific probe but not as the best prover.</div>
<div id="LC654" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC655" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">This suggests maintaining at least two scoreboards:</div>
<div id="LC656" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC657" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Proof-search scoreboard:</span>} success, expansion discovery cost, wall time, memory, robustness, proof quality.</div>
<div id="LC658" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> <span class="pl-c1">\textbf</span>{<span class="pl-mb">Creative-discovery scoreboard:</span>} normalized novelty, creative yield, anytime creative efficiency, reusable lemma yield, route diversity.</div>
<div id="LC659" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC660" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div></div>
<div class="react-no-virtualization-wrapper"><div id="LC661" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The best architecture may lie on a Pareto frontier rather than at the maximum of either scoreboard alone.</div>
<div id="LC662" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC663" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Research Commitments for the Next Data-ATP Iteration</span>}</div>
<div id="LC664" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC665" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Before the next creativity-enabled run, the project should commit to the following private experimental rules:</div>
<div id="LC666" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC667" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Creativity changes search allocation, never formal acceptance.</div>
<div id="LC668" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> The user-facing scalar control is not itself called measured creativity in the data analysis.</div>
<div id="LC669" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Total resources are frozen or explicitly charged; higher creativity does not silently buy more computation.</div>
<div id="LC670" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Reference-route information is separated into explicit information conditions <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>K_<span class="pl-c1">0</span>,K_<span class="pl-c1">1</span>,K_<span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-pds">$</span></span>.</div>
<div id="LC671" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Novelty is scored only on verifier-accepted, normalized proofs.</div>
<div id="LC672" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Seeds and target partitions are declared before outcomes are inspected.</div>
<div id="LC673" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Current <span class="pl-c1">\texttt</span>{<span class="pl-c1">sgrpcl</span>} results are archived as baseline observations, not retrofitted into a matched experiment.</div>
<div id="LC674" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Failed searches, bounded unknowns, crashes, and replay events are part of the dataset.</div>
<div id="LC675" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Conjectures in this document remain conjectures until the declared test actually supports them.</div>
<div id="LC676" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-k">\item</span> Architecture complexity must earn its way in through measured gains.</div>
<div id="LC677" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">enumerate</span>}</div>
<div id="LC678" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC679" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Conclusion</span>}</div>
<div id="LC680" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC681" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The central idea can be stated without anthropomorphism. Formal proof search is a resource-allocation problem over a vast set of legal possibilities. Learning supplies a prior ordering of attention. Creativity controls how willing the system is to depart from that ordering, search alternative neighborhoods, construct intermediate lemmas, restart, or preserve multiple competing theories of the proof. Verification remains unchanged.</div>
<div id="LC682" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC683" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The mathematical results in this draft already rule out several tempting mistakes. Maximum temperature need not be best. Entropy does not uniquely determine creativity. Novelty alone can select a worse prover. Randomly mixing creative personalities cannot outperform the best constituent in expectation unless the architecture adds something more. Adaptive policies are theoretically worth considering because they contain fixed policies as special cases, but their actual benefit remains empirical. Counterfactual exploration has a clean rescue mechanism when a proof-enabling action lies outside an ordinary candidate cap.</div>
<div id="LC684" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC685" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The near-term Data-ATP question is therefore precise:</div>
<div id="LC686" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">center</span>}</div>
<div id="LC687" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\fcolorbox</span>{DataBlue}{DataLight}{<span class="pl-c1">\parbox</span>{0.86<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}{<span class="pl-c1">\centering\bfseries</span> Which forms and amounts of structured exploration turn a fixed budget into better verified discovery?}}</div>
<div id="LC688" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">center</span>}</div>
<div id="LC689" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The right answer may be moderate creativity, adaptive creativity, a portfolio of cooperating agents, learned proof geometry, or none of these on a given benchmark. The point of D-A-T-P-CREAT-01 is to make that answer something the system can measure rather than something the architecture assumes.</div>
<div id="LC690" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC691" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\clearpage</span></div>
<div id="LC692" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\appendix</span></div>
<div id="LC693" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\section</span>{<span class="pl-en">Metric Summary</span>}</div>
<div id="LC694" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC695" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\begin</span>{<span class="pl-smi">longtable</span>}{@{}p{0.25<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}p{0.27<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}p{0.40<span class="pl-c1">\textwidth</span>}@{}}</div>
<div id="LC696" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\toprule</span></div>
<div id="LC697" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Quantity &amp; Definition &amp; Interpretation<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC698" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\midrule</span></div>
<div id="LC699" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\endhead</span></div>
<div id="LC700" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Control index &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>c_F(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\sum</span>_i<span class="pl-c1">\alpha</span>_i z_i(u_i)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Exploratory setting summary; input, not outcome.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC701" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Success probability &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>D(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\Prb</span>(S_u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Probability of a verified proof inside the frozen protocol.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC702" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Conditional novelty &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>N(u)=<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>(P_u)<span class="pl-c1">\mid</span> S_u]<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Expected structural novelty among successful runs.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC703" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Creative yield &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)=D(u)N(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Expected verified novelty per run.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC704" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Scarcity-weighted yield &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>R_F(<span class="pl-c1">\Gamma</span>,<span class="pl-c1">\varphi</span>)<span class="pl-c1">\cY</span>(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Target-relative weighting; useful mainly across targets.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC705" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Discovery cost &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span><span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> or expansion count &amp; Work before first verifier-accepted proof.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC706" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Anytime creative efficiency &amp; <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>T^{-1}<span class="pl-c1">\E</span>[<span class="pl-c1">\nu</span>_u(T-<span class="pl-c1">\tau</span>_u)_+]<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Rewards novel proofs that are found earlier.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC707" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Overall utility &amp; declared <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$</span>U(u)<span class="pl-pds">$</span></span> &amp; Multi-objective role-dependent ATP quality.<span class="pl-c1">\\</span></div>
<div id="LC708" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\bottomrule</span></div>
<div id="LC709" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div "><span class="pl-c1">\end</span>{<span class="pl-smi">longtable</span>}</div>

</div>

    <style>
      .user-mention[href$="/btenneson"] {
        color: var(--color-user-mention-fg);
        background-color: var(--bgColor-attention-muted, var(--color-attention-subtle));
        border-radius: 2px;
        margin-left: -2px;
        margin-right: -2px;
      }
      .user-mention[href$="/btenneson"]:before,
      .user-mention[href$="/btenneson"]:after {
        content: '';
        display: inline-block;
        width: 2px;
      }
    </style>
